> तिस्रस्त्रिप्रकारा उत्तममध्यमाधमभावेन त्रिविधा द्यावो द्युलोका अस्मिन्वरुणेऽंतर्मध्यॆ निहिताः । तिस्रः पूववस्त्रिविधा भूमीर्भूम्यश्च षड्विधानाः । विधानं

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The three heavens, of the threefold kind (highest, middle and lowest), are placed within this Varuṇa, in his midst. The three earths likewise, of the same threefold kind, are "षड्विधानाः (*ṣaḍvidhānāḥ*)": विधानं (*vidhānaṃ*) is विधा (*vidhā*)

### Printed p. 72

> विधा । वसंताद्यतुभेदेन षड्विधाः प्रकारा यासु तादृश्यः । उपरा अस्मिन्नेव वरुण
> उप्ता अंतर्भूताः । लोकानां त्रित्वं च त्रयो ना इमे त्रिवृतो लोका इत्यादिना ब्राह्मणेनावगम्यते । तिस्रो भूमीर्धारयन् । ऋ. २-२७-८ । इति निगमश्च
> भवति । इमा लोकावावृत्य वरुणस्तिष्ठतीत्यर्थः । अपि च गृत्सः स्तुत्यो राजेश्वरः स
> वरुणो दिव्यंतरिक्षॆ हिरण्मयं हिरण्मयं सुवर्णमयं हितरमणीयं वा प्रेंखं
> दोलावद्दिग्वयसंस्पर्शिनमेतं सूर्यं शुभे कं दीप्त्यर्थं चक्रे । कृतवान् ।
> दिवि सूर्यमदधात्तोममद्रौ । ऋ. ५-८५-२ । इति हि श्रूयते ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The earths have six kinds according to the seasons, spring and the rest. They are "उपराः (*uparāḥ*)", sown (upta) in this very Varuṇa, contained in him. That the worlds are three is known from a Brāhmaṇa passage beginning त्रयो ना इमे त्रिवृतो लोका (*trayo nā ime trivṛto lokā*). There is also a Nigama passage, तिस्रो भूमीर्धारयन् (*tisro bhūmīrdhārayan*) (Ṛ. 2-27-8). The sense is that Varuṇa stands encompassing these worlds. Moreover Varuṇa, the praised king and lord, has made in the sky this sun, golden or delightful to the beloved, touching the directions like a swing, for brightness. For it is heard in the Ṛk दिवि सूर्यमदधात्तोममद्रौ (*divi sūryamadadhāttomamadrau*) (Ṛ. 5-85-2).

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- तिस्रः (*tisraḥ*)—having three kinds: highest, middle and lowest
- द्यावः (*dyāvaḥ*)—the heavens
- अस्मिन् (*asmin*)—of this same Varuṇa
- अंतः (*aṃtaḥ*)—in the inner region
- निहिताः (*nihitāḥ*)—are established
- तिस्रः (*tisraḥ*)—having three varieties of this same kind
- भूमीः (*bhūmīḥ*)—the earths also
- षड्विधानाः (*ṣaḍvidhānāḥ*)—together with the six seasons beginning with spring
- उपराः (*uparāḥ*)—are contained in this very Varuṇa
- गृतस्यः (*gṛtasyaḥ*)—the praised
- राजा (*rājā*)—and lordly
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—god Varuṇa
- हिरण्ययं (*hiraṇyayaṃ*)—golden
- प्रेंखं (*preṃkhaṃ*)—swinging to both directions like a swing
- एतं (*etaṃ*)—this sun
- शुभे कं (*śubhe kaṃ*)—to spread brightness
- दिवि (*divi*)—in the sky
- चक्रे (*cakre*)—has established

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

The heavens, of the three kinds highest, middle and lowest, are established in the inner region of this same Varuṇa. In the same way the earths, of three varieties, are contained in this Varuṇa together with the six seasons beginning with spring. The praised and lordly god Varuṇa has established in the sky, to spread brightness, this sun, which is golden and swings to both directions like a swing.

##### English translation
In him are deposited the three heavens, the three earths with their six seasons are sown in him : the most adorable, royal Varuna has made this golden sun undulating in the sky, he has made it to diffuse light.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अव॒ सिंधुं॒ वरु॑णो॒ द्यौरि॑व स्थाद्द्र॒प्पो न श्वे॒तो मृ॒गस्तुवि॑ष्मान् ।  
> 72 सायणभाव्यसहिता [ मं. ७. अ. ५. सू. ८७.  
> गं॒धी॒रशं॑सो॒ रज॑सो वि॒मानः॑ सुपा॒रक्ष॑त्रः स॒तो अ॒स्य राजा॑॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अव । सिंधुं । वरुणः । द्यौः इव । स्थात् । द्रप्सः । न । श्वेतः । मृगः । तुविष्मान् ।  
> गंभीरशंसः । रजसः । विमानः । सुपार्क्षत्रः । सतः । अस्य । राजा ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> वारुणे पशावव सिंधुमिति वपाया अनुवाक्या । सूत्रितं च । अव सिंधुं
> वरुणो दौरिव स्थादयं सु तुभ्यं वरुण स्वधावः । आ. ३-७ । इति ॥
> दौरिव सूर्य इव दीपो वरुणः सिंधुं समुद्रमव स्थात् । वेलायामवस्थापयति । यथा वेलां नातिक्रामति तथा करोतीत्यर्थः । कीदृशो वरुणः ।
> द्रप्पो न द्रवणशील उदबिंदुरिव श्वेतः शुभ्रवर्णो मृगः । लुप्तोपममेतत् ।
> गौरमृग इव तुविष्टान् बलवान् प्रवृद्दो वा गंभीरशंसः । गंभीरो महाइंभंसः स्तोत्रं यस्य स तथोक्तः । रजस उदकस्य विमानो निर्माता सुपारक्षत्रः
> सुष्टु दुःखात्पारकं क्षत्रं बलं धनं वा यस्य तादृशो वरुणः सतो विद्यमानस्यास्य जगतो राजेश्वरो भवति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Use: in the animal offering to Varuṇa, this Ṛk is the anuvākyā for the vapā offering; the sūtra says: अव सिंधुं वरुणो द्यौरिव स्थाद्द्रप्पो न श्वेतो मृगस्तुविष्मान् । 72 सायणभाव्यसहिता [ मं. ७. अ. ५. सू. ८७. गंधीरशंसो रजसो विमानः सुपारक्षत्रः सतो अस्य राजा॥ अव । सिंधुं । वरुणः । द्यौः इव । स्थात् । द्रप्सः । न । श्वेतः । मृगः । तुविष्मान् । गंभीरशंसः । रजसः । विमानः । सुपार्क्षत्रः । सतः । अस्य । राजा ॥ ६ ॥ वारुणे पशावव सिंधुमिति वपाया अनुवाक्या । सूत्रितं च । अव सिंधुं वरुणो दौरिव स्थादयं सु तुभ्यं वरुण स्वधावः । (*ava siṃdhuṃ varuṇo dyauriva sthāddrappo na śveto mṛgastuviṣmān | 72 sāyaṇabhāvyasahitā [ maṃ. 7. a. 5. sū. 87. gaṃdhīraśaṃso rajaso vimānaḥ supārakṣatraḥ sato asya rājā‖ ava | siṃdhuṃ | varuṇaḥ | dyauḥ iva | sthāt | drapsaḥ | na | śvetaḥ | mṛgaḥ | tuviṣmān | gaṃbhīraśaṃsaḥ | rajasaḥ | vimānaḥ | supārkṣatraḥ | sataḥ | asya | rājā ‖ 6 ‖ vāruṇe paśāvava siṃdhumiti vapāyā anuvākyā | sūtritaṃ ca | ava siṃdhuṃ varuṇo dauriva sthādayaṃ su tubhyaṃ varuṇa svadhāvaḥ |*) (Ā. 3-7). Meaning: Varuṇa, bright like the sun ("dyau"), has made the ocean stand in its bounds; he so acts that it does not cross its shore. What is Varuṇa like? He is white-coloured like a flowing drop of water ("drapsa"), and like a gaura deer he is strong ("तुविष्मान् (*tuviṣmān*)") or grown great. He is "गंभीरशंसः (*gaṃbhīraśaṃsaḥ*)", one whose praise is deep and great; the maker of water; "सुपारक्षत्रः (*supārakṣatraḥ*)", one whose strength or wealth carries well across sorrow. Such Varuṇa is the king and lord of this existing world.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- द्रप्पो न (*drappo na*)—like a drop of water
- श्वेतः (*śvetaḥ*)—white-coloured
- मृगः (*mṛgaḥ*)—like the gaura deer
- तुविष्मान् (*tuviṣmān*)—of great strength
- गंभीरशंसः (*gaṃbhīraśaṃsaḥ*)—worthy of great praise
- रजसः (*rajasaḥ*)—of water
- विमानः (*vimānaḥ*)—the maker
- सुपारक्षत्रः (*supārakṣatraḥ*)—of the strength to carry across sorrow
- सतः (*sataḥ*)—of the nature of being
- अस्य (*asya*)—of this whole world
- राजा (*rājā*)—the lord
- द्यौः इव (*dyauḥ iva*)—and bright like the sun
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- सिंधुं (*siṃdhuṃ*)—the ocean
- अव स्थात् (*ava sthāt*)—has restrained and established so that it does not cross its shore

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Varuṇa is white-coloured like a drop of water, of great strength like the gaura deer, worthy of great praise, the maker of water, possessed of the strength to carry across sorrow, the lord of this whole world, which is of the nature of being, and bright like the sun. He has restrained and established the ocean so that it does not cross its shore.

### Printed p. 73

##### English translation
(Radiant) as the sun, Varuna placed the ocean (in its bed), white as a drop (of water), vigorous as an antelope, objects of profound praise, distributor of water, the powerful transporter beyond sin, the ruler of this existing (world).

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the animal offering made to Varuṇa, this Ṛk, अव सिंधुं (*ava siṃdhuṃ*), is to be recited as the anuvākyā mantra in the vapā offering. This is set out by the sūtra of the Āśvalāyana Śrautasūtra, अव सिंधुं वरुणो द्यौरिव स्थादयं सु तुभ्यं वरुण स्वधावः (*ava siṃdhuṃ varuṇo dyauriva sthādayaṃ su tubhyaṃ varuṇa svadhāvaḥ*) (Ā. 3-7).

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यो मृ॒ळया॑ति च॒क्रुषे॑ चि॒दागो॑ व॒यं स्या॑म॒ वरु॑णे॒  ॑
> अनागाः ।  
> अनु॑ व्र॒तान्यदि॑तेर्॒ऋधंतो॑ यू॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । मृळयाति । चक्रुषे चित् । आगः । वयं । स्याम । वरुणे । अनागाः ।  
> अनु । व्रतानि । अदितेः । ऋधंतः । यूयं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥ ७ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> आगोऽपराधं चक्रुषे चित्यतवतेऽपि स्तोत्रे यो वरुणो मृळयाति
> उदयां करोति तस्मिन्वरुणेऽनागा अनागसोऽनपराधाः संतो वयं स्याम ।
> वर्तमाना भवेम । किं कुर्वंतः । अदितेरदीनस्य वरुणस्य संबंधीनि व्रतानि कर्माण्यन्वानुपूर्व्यणर्धंतः समर्धयंतः । हे वरुणादयो देवाः यूयमस्मान्सर्वदा कल्याणैः पालयत ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The Varuṇa who shows mercy even to a worshipper who has committed an offence, may we be blameless towards that Varuṇa. Doing what? Making the rites ("व्रतानि (*vratāni*)") of Aditi's, that is the undiminished, Varuṇa grow in due order. O Varuṇa and the other gods, may you always protect us with blessings.

### Printed p. 74

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- आगः (*āgaḥ*)—an offence
- चक्रुषे चित् (*cakruṣe cit*)—even towards the man who has committed
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Varuṇa who
- मृळयति (*mṛḷayati*)—shows mercy and makes him happy
- वरुणे (*varuṇe*)—in regard to such a Varuṇa
- अदितेः (*aditeḥ*)—of that same Varuṇa, son of Aditi
- व्रतानि (*vratāni*)—the rites of worship
- अनु (*anu*)—in due order
- ऋधंतः (*ṛdhaṃtaḥ*)—increasing (performing in greater measure)
- वयं (*vayaṃ*)—we
- अनागाः (*anāgāḥ*)—free from sin
- स्याम (*syāma*)—let us be (O Varuṇa and the other gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Even towards the man who has committed an offence, Varuṇa shows mercy and makes him happy. In regard to such a Varuṇa, may we be free from sin and keep increasing in due order the rites of worship of that same Varuṇa, son of Aditi. O Varuṇa and the other gods, always protect us with blessings.

##### English translation
May we be free from sin against that Varuna, who has compassion upon him who commits offence, we who are duly observing the rites of the son of Aditi: and do you, (gods), ever cherish us with blessings.

## Sūkta 88 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> प्र शुंध्युवमिति सप्तर्चमष्टादशं सूक्तं वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं नारुणं ।
> प्र शुंध्युवमित्यनुक्रांतं ॥ गतो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sūkta प्र शुंध्युवं (*pra śuṃdhyuvaṃ*) has seven Ṛks. Its seer is Vasiṣṭha, its deity Varuṇa and its metre Triṣṭubh. The Anukramaṇī says प्र शुंध्युवं (*pra śuṃdhyuvaṃ*). The use (viniyoga) has already been stated.

Anuvāda: this sūkta, प्र शुंध्युवं (*pra śuṃdhyuvaṃ*), is the eighteenth sūkta in the fifth anuvāka. It has seven Ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is its seer, Varuṇa its deity, and its metre is Triṣṭubh. In the Anukramaṇī it is stated as प्र शुंध्युवं (*pra śuṃdhyuvaṃ*). The use was stated earlier.

### Printed p. 75

Sūkta 88. Maṇḍala 7. Anuvāka 5. Sūkta 88. Aṣṭaka 5. Adhyāya 6. Varga 10. Number of Ṛks in the sūkta: 7. Seer: Vasiṣṭha. Deity: Varuṇa. Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र शुं॒ध्युवं॒ वरु॑णाय॒ प्रेष्ठां॑ म॒तिं व॑सिष्ठ मा॒ळ्हुषे॑  
> भरस्व ।  
> य ई॑म॒र्वांचं॒ कर॑ते॒ यज॑त्रं स॒हस्रा॑मघं॒ वृष॑णं  ॒॑
> बृहंतं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । शुंध्युवं । वरुणाय । प्रेष्ठां । मतिं । वसिष्ठ । माळ्हुषे । भरस्व ।  
> यः । ईं । अर्वांचं । करते । यजत्रं । सहस्रमघं । वृषणं । बृहंतं ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ऋषिरात्मानमेव प्रत्यक्षीकृत्य स्तुता नियुंक्ते । हे वसिष्ठ त्वं मीळ्हुषे
> सेक्त्रे वरुणाय शुंध्युवं शोधयित्रीं यद्वा स्वत एव शुद्धां प्रेष्ठां प्रियतमां मतिं मननीयामीदृशीं स्तुतिं प्र भरस्व । प्रहर । प्रापय । यो वरुण
> ईमेनं सूर्यमर्वांचमस्मदभिमुखं करते अंतरिक्षे करोति तस्मै वरुणायेत्यन्वयः । कीदृशं सूर्यं । यजत्रं यष्टव्यं सहस्रामघं बहुधनं वृषणं
> कामानां वर्षकं बृहंतं महांतं !

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The seer, making himself present, urges himself in praise. O Vasiṣṭha, bring forth to Varuṇa, the showerer ("मीळ्हुषे (*mīḷhuṣe*)"), a hymn ("mati"), worthy of thought, that is purifying (śundhyu), or else pure of itself, and most dear. The construction is: to that Varuṇa who ("ईं (*īṃ*)") makes this sun face towards us, places it in the sky. What kind of sun? Worthy to be worshipped ("yajatra"), of a thousand-fold wealth, showerer of desires, great.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वसिष्ठ (*vasiṣṭha*)—O seer Vasiṣṭha
- यजत्रं (*yajatraṃ*)—worthy of worship
- सहस्रामघं (*sahasrāmaghaṃ*)—possessed of exceedingly great wealth
- वृषणं (*vṛṣaṇaṃ*)—the showerer of rain
- बृहंतं (*bṛhaṃtaṃ*)—of great nature
- ईं (*īṃ*)—

### Printed p. 76
- …—this sun
- अर्वांचं (*arvāṃcaṃ*)—facing us
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which Varuṇa
- करते (*karate*)—establishes in the atmosphere (such a Varuṇa)
- मीळ्हुषे (*mīḷhuṣe*)—to the showerer of desires
- वरुणाय (*varuṇāya*)—addressing Varuṇa
- शुंध्युवं (*śuṃdhyuvaṃ*)—pure in itself
- प्रेष्ठां (*preṣṭhāṃ*)—dearest to him
- मतिं (*matiṃ*)—hymn of praise
- प्र भरस्व (*pra bharasva*)—offer

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O sage Vasiṣṭha, offer a hymn of praise that is pure in itself and dearest to him, addressed to Varuṇa, the showerer of desires, who establishes in the atmosphere, facing us, this Sun, who is worshipful, possessed of abundant wealth, a sender of rain, and of great form.

##### English translation
Offer pure and acceptable praise, Vasishta, to the showerer, Varuna, he who makes the adorable (sun), the donor of thousands, the showerer (of benefits), the vast, manifest before (us).

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अधा॒ न्व॑स्य सं॒दृशं॑ जग॒न्वान॒ग्नेरनी॑कं॒ वरु॑णस्य मंसि ।  
> स्व१॒॑गर्यदश्म॑न्नधि॒पा उ॒ आंधो॒ भि मा॒ वपु॑र्दृ॒शये॑ निनीयात् ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अध । नु । अस्य । संदृशं । जगन्वान् । अग्नेः । अनीकं । वरुणस्य । मंसि ।  
> स्वः । यत् । अश्मन् । अधिपाः । ऊं इति । अंधः । अभि । मा । वपुः । दृशये ।  
> निनीयात् ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अधाधुनास्य वरुणस्य संदृशं संदर्शनं नु क्षिप्रं जगन्वान् गतवानहमग्नेरनीकं ज्वालासंघं मंसि । स्तवानि । तं वरुणं यष्टुमिति शेषः । यद्यदा वरुणः
> स्वः सुखकरमश्मनश्चन्यभिषवार्थे पाषाणेवस्थितं । अभिषुतमित्यर्थः । ईदृशमंधः

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"Now" (अधाधुना (*adhādhunā*)), I have quickly come to the sight (संदृशं (*saṃdṛśaṃ*)) of this Varuṇa, and I praise the mass of flames of Agni (*agneranīkaṃ*); the words "in order to worship that Varuṇa" are to be supplied. "When" (यद्यदा (*yadyadā*)) Varuṇa… स्वः (*svaḥ*) is the pleasant (*sukhakaraṃ*), that which lies on the stone (अश्मन् (*aśman*)) used for pressing, that is, the pressed Soma. The sentence is completed in the next block.

### Printed p. 77

> सोमलक्षणमन्नमधिपा अधिकं पाता भवेत् ॥ छांदसः षष्ठ्यभावः ॥ उ इति पूरकः।
> तदानीं मा मां वपुः शरीरं स्वकीयं । वपुरिति रूनाम । प्रशस्तं रूपं वा
> दृशये दर्शनार्थमभि निनीयात् । अभिप्राययेत् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The अंधः (*aṃdhaḥ*) is Soma-food. Varuṇa would be its "greater drinker" (*adhipā adhikaṃ pātā bhavet*); the absence of the genitive is Vedic usage. उ (*u*) is a filler. Then may he lead my own body (वपुः (*vapuḥ*)—"vapus" is a name for form, or else an excellent form) towards being seen (दृशये (*dṛśaye*)), that is, may he bring it to view. **NOTE**

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अध (*adha*)—in the present context
- अस्य वरुणस्य (*asya varuṇasya*)—of this same Varuṇa
- संदृशं (*saṃdṛśaṃ*)—the most excellent form
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- जगन्वान् (*jaganvān*)—I who have approached
- अग्नेः (*agneḥ*)—of his flame-form
- अनेकं (*anekaṃ*)—the mass
- मंसि (*maṃsi*)—I praise (or: I praise the flames of Agni for the worship of Varuṇa)
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- स्वः (*svaḥ*)—the pleasant
- आश्मन् (*āśman*)—pressed by stones
- अंधः (*aṃdhaḥ*)—the food in the form of Soma
- अधिपाः (*adhipāḥ*)—drinks (then)
- मां (*māṃ*)—my
- वपुः (*vapuḥ*)—body
- दृशये (*dṛśaye*)—with attractive splendour
- अभि निनीयात् (*abhi ninīyāt*)—may he join

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

In the present context, I who have at once beheld in person the most excellent form of this same Varuṇa, further praise the mass of radiance in the form of flame. When Varuṇa drinks the pleasant Soma-food, crushed and pressed by stones, then may he join my body with attractive splendour.

##### English translation
Hastening into his sight, may I (worthily) glorify the aggregated radiance of Varuna, when he is the imbiber of the exhilarating beverage (expressed) by the stones: may he render my person of goodly aspect.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> आ यद्रु॒हाव॒ वरु॑णश्च॒ नावं॒ प्र यत्स॑मु॒द्रमी॒रया॑व॒  ॑
> मध्यं ।  
> अधि॒ यद॒पां स्नुभि॒श्चरा॑व॒ प्र प्रें॒ख ईं॑खयावहै शु॒भे कं॥

### Printed p. 78

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> आ । यत् । रुहाव । वरुणः । च । नावं । प्र । यत् । समुद्रं । ईरयाव ।  
> मध्यं ।  
> अधि । यत् । अपां । स्मृभिः । चराव । प्र । प्रकंखे । ईंखयावहै । शुभे कं॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> यद्यदा वरुणे प्रसन्ने सत्यहं वरुणश्चोभौ नावं द्रुममयिां तेणसाधनभूतामा रुहाव उभवारूढावभूव । तां च नावं यद्यदा समुद्रं मध्यं
> समुद्रस्य मध्यं प्रति प्रेरयाव प्रकर्षण गमयाव । यद्यदा चापामुदकानामध्युपरि स्नुभिर्गंत्रीभिरन्याभिरपि नौभिश्चराव वर्तावहै । तदानीं शुभे शोभार्थं
> प्रेंखे नौरूपाया. दोलायावॆ (व प्रेंख ० र्वास्य निम्मोन्नतैस्तरंगैरितश्चतश्च
> प्रविचलंतौ संक्रीडावहै । कमिति पूरकः । यद्वा । क्रियाविशेषणं । कं सुखं यथा
> भवति तथेत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"When" Varuṇa is pleased, I and Varuṇa, both of us, board the ship (नावं (*nāvaṃ*)) made of wood, the means of crossing; and "when" we drive it forward into the middle of the sea; and "when" we move over the waters (अपां (*apāṃ*)) with other swift (स्नुभिः (*snubhiḥ*), glossed *gaṃtrībhiḥ*) ships, then, for splendour, we sport together in the swing (प्रेंखे (*preṃkhe*)) that is the ship, moving hither and thither on the low and high waves. कं (*kaṃ*) is a filler; or else it is an adverb: "in such a way that there is happiness."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यत् (*yat*)—when
- वरुणश्च (*varuṇaśca*)—(I) and Varuṇa, both together
- नावं (*nāvaṃ*)—the ship made of wood
- आ रुहाव (*ā ruhāva*)—we two board
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- समुद्रं मध्यं (*samudraṃ madhyaṃ*)—in the middle of the ocean that is the atmosphere
- प्र ईरयाव (*pra īrayāva*)—we go forward greatly
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- अपां (*apāṃ*)—of the waters
- अधि (*adhi*)—upon the surface
- स्नुभिः (*snubhiḥ*)—with ships that move about
- चराव (*carāva*)—we two move about, then
- शुभे (*śubhe*)—in the auspicious
- प्रेंखे (*preṃkhe*)—in the swing in the form of a ship
- कं (*kaṃ*)—happily
- प्र ईंखयावहै (*pra īṃkhayāvahai*)—we make a sportive oscillation (we swing)

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

When I (Vasiṣṭha) and Varuṇa together board the ship made of wood, when we go forward greatly in the middle of the ocean that is the atmosphere, when we move about for sport with ships that move on the surface of the waters, then in the auspicious swing in the form of a ship we happily make a sportive oscillation.

##### English translation
Waen (I Vasishtha) and Varuna, ascend the ship together, when we send it forth into the midst of the ocean, when we proceed over the

### Printed p. 80
waters with swift (sailing vessels), then may we both undulate happily in the prosperous swing.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> वसि॑ष्ठं ह॒ वरु॑णो ना॒व्याधा॒दृषिं॑ चकार॒ स्वपा॒ महो॑भिः ।  
> स्तो॒तारं॒ विप्रः॑ सुदिन॒त्वे अह्नां॒ यान्नु द्याव॑स्त॒तन॒न्यादु॒षासः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> वसिष्ठं । ह । वरुणः । नावि । आ । अधात् । ऋषिं । चकार । सुअपाः । महःभिः ।  
> स्तोतारं । विप्रः । सुदिनत्वे । अह्नां । यात् । नु । द्यावः । ततनन् । यात् ।  
> उषसः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> एवं वसिष्ठेनात्मनोक्ते यद्वरुणेन कृतं तद्दर्शयति । वसिष्ठं ह वसिष्ठं खलु
> वरुणो नावि स्वकीयायामाधात् । अरोहयत् । तथा तम्मृषिमवोभी रक्षणैः
> स्वपां स्वपसं शोभनकर्माणं चकार । वरुणः कृतवान् । अपि च विप्रो मेधावी
> वरुणोऽह्नां दिवसानां मध्यॆ सुदिनत्वे यत्पलत्वेन शोभनदिनत्वं तत्र स्तोतारमस्थापयदिति शेषः । किं कुर्वन् । याद्यातो गच्छतो द्यावो दिवसान् याद्यातीरुषास उषसोपलक्षिता रात्रीश्च नु क्षिप्रं ततनन् सूर्यात्मना विस्तारयन् ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Vasiṣṭha having thus spoken of himself, Sāyaṇa shows what Varuṇa did. Varuṇa placed Vasiṣṭha in his own ship (नावि (*nāvi*)), made him board it. Likewise he made that seer स्वपां (*svapāṃ*), one of good works, through protections (*avobhī rakṣaṇaiḥ*). And the wise Varuṇa, among the days, set the worshipper in a "good day" (सुदिनत्वे (*sudinatve*)), that is, a day that is good by its fruit. How? By quickly extending, in the form of the sun, the passing days and the passing nights marked by dawns.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—Varuṇa
- वसिष्ठं ह (*vasiṣṭhaṃ ha*)—Vasiṣṭha
- नावि (*nāvi*)—in his own ship
- आधात् (*ādhāt*)—seated, having made him board
- महोभिः (*mahobhiḥ*)—with great protections
- ऋषिं (*ṛṣiṃ*)—that same seer Vasiṣṭha
- स्वपां (*svapāṃ*)—as one with excellent works
- चकार (*cakāra*)—made
- विप्रः (*vipraḥ*)—the wise Varuṇa
- यात् (*yāt*)—passing
- द्यावः (*dyāvaḥ*)—days
- यात् (*yāt*)—passing
- उषासः (*uṣāsaḥ*)—nights marked by dawns
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- ततनन् (*tatanan*)—making extended
- अह्नां (*ahnāṃ*)—among all the days
- सुदिनत्वे (*sudinatve*)—on the most auspicious days
- स्तोतारं (*stotāraṃ*)—the praiser Vasiṣṭha he placed

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Varuṇa made Vasiṣṭha board his own ship and be seated, and made that same seer one of excellent works through his great protections. The wise Varuṇa, extending the passing days and the passing nights marked by dawns, placed the praiser Vasiṣṭha among the most auspicious of all days.

##### English translation
So Varuna placed Vasishtha in the ship, and by his mighty protection made the Rishi a doer of good works: the wise Varuna placed his worshipper in a fortunate day of days, he extended the passing days, the passing nights.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> क्व१॒ं॑ त्यानि॑ नौ स॒ख्या ब॑भूवुः॒ सचा॑वहे॒ यद॑वृ॒कं पु॒रा चि॑त् ।  
> बृ॒हंतं॒ मानं॑ वरुण स्वधावः स॒हस्र॑द्वारं जगमा गृ॒हं ते॑ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> क्व । त्यानि । नौ । सख्या । बभूवुः । सचावहे इति । यत् । अवृकं । पुरा । चित् ।  
> बृहंतं । मानं । वरुण । स्वध्वावः । सहस्रद्वारं । जगम । गृहं । ते ॥ ५ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वरुण त्यानि तानि पुरातनानि नावावयोः सख्या सख्यानि सखित्वानि क्व कुत्र
> बभूवुः । पुरा पूर्वस्मिन्कालेऽवृकमहिंस्यमात्यंतिकं यत्सख्यमस्ति तत्सचावहे ।
> आवां सेवावहे । चिदिति पूरकः । अपि च हे स्वधावोऽन्नवन्नवरुण ते त्वदीयं गृहं
> जगम । गच्छानि ॥ लोडर्थे लिट् ॥ कीदृशं गृहं । बृहंतं महांतं मानं ।
> मांत्यस्मिन्सर्वाणि भूतानि मानु । सर्वस्य भूतजातस्य परिच्छेदकमित्यर्थः । सहस्रद्वारं बहुद्वारं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Varuṇa, where have those ancient friendships of us two (सख्या (*sakhyā*)) gone? The unhurt, lasting friendship that existed in an earlier time, सचावहे (*sacāvahe*): we two serve it (चित् (*cit*) is a filler). And, O Varuṇa, possessor of food (स्वधावः (*svadhāvaḥ*)), may I go to your house (जगम (*jagama*), a perfect in the sense of the imperative). What house? Great (बृहंतं (*bṛhaṃtaṃ*)); मानं (*mānaṃ*) because in it all beings are measured, that is, it defines all creatures; thousand-doored, that is, many-doored.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Varuṇa) नौ (*nau*)—between us two
- त्यानि (*tyāni*)—those formerly well known
- सख्या (*sakhyā*)—friendship
- क्व (*kva*)—where
- बभूवुः (*babhūvuḥ*)—has it gone into hiding
- पुरा चित् (*purā cit*)—all of old
- अवृकं (*avṛkaṃ*)—between us two, very

### Printed p. 81
- …—intimate
- यत् (*yat*)—whatever friendship there was, that
- सचावहे (*sacāvahe*)—let us enjoy again
- स्वधावः (*svadhāvaḥ*)—possessor of food
- वरुण (*varuṇa*)—O Varuṇa
- बृहतं (*bṛhataṃ*)—great
- मानं (*mānaṃ*)—the support of all beings
- सहस्रद्वारं (*sahasradvāraṃ*)—having a thousand doors
- ते गृहं (*te gṛhaṃ*)—to your house
- जगम (*jagama*)—may I come and arrive

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, where has our friendship, formerly so well known, gone now? Let us now again enjoy that very intimate friendship that was between us two of old. O Varuṇa, possessor of food, may I come and arrive at your house, which is great, the support of all beings, and has a thousand doors.

##### English translation
What has become of those our ancient friendships? let us preserve them unimpaired as of old: food-bestowing Varuna, may I repair to your vast comprehensive thousand-doored dwelling.

#### Ṛk 6

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> य आ॒पिर्नित्यो॑ वरुण प्रि॒यः सन्त्वामागां॑सि कृ॒णव॒त्सखा॑ ते ।  
> मा त॒ एन॑स्वंतो यक्षिन्भुजेम यं॒धि ष्मा॒ विप्रः॑ स्तुव॒ते  ॑
> वरूढं ॥ ६ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यः । आपिः । नित्यः । वरुण । प्रियः । सन् । त्वां । आगांसि । कृणवत् । सखा । ते ।  
> मा । ते । एनस्वंतः । यक्षिन् । भुजेम । यंधि । स्म । विप्रः । स्तुवते । वरूथं ॥६॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वरुण यो वसिष्ठो नित्यो ध्रुव आपिर्बंधुः । औरसः पुत्र इत्यर्थः ।
> यः पूर्वं प्रियः संस्तां प्रत्यागांस्य पराधान् कृणवत् अकरोत् स इदानीं ते
> तव सखा समानख्यानः प्रियोऽस्तु । हे यक्षिन्यजनीय वरुण ते तव स्वभूता
> वयमेनस्वंत एनसा पापेन युक्ताः संतो मा भुजेम । मा भुज्महि । त्वत्प-
> [ 6 ]                                                                 [ Vol. 23 ]

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

O Varuṇa, the Vasiṣṭha who is your constant, lasting kinsman (आपिः (*āpiḥ*): a relative, that is, a son of your own body), who, though formerly dear, committed offences (आगांसि (*āgāṃsi*)) against you, let him now be your dear friend, of the same fame. O adorable Varuṇa, we who are your own, being afflicted with sin (एनस्वंतः (*enasvaṃtaḥ*)), may we not enjoy (मा भुजेम (*mā bhujema*)). The next words of the block are damaged and the sentence continues in the following block. **NOTE**

### Printed p. 82

> साधात्वा परहिता एव संतो भोगान् भुनजामहृ । विप्रो मेधावी त्वं च स्तुवते
> कुर्वते वसिष्ठाय वरूथमनिप्पनिवारकं वरणीयं वा गृहं यंधि ।
> प्रयच्छ + स्वेति पूरकः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

And you, the wise (विप्रः (*vipraḥ*)), give (यंधि (*yaṃdhi*), that is प्रयच्छ (*prayaccha*); स्म (*sma*) is a filler) to Vasiṣṭha, who praises (स्तुवते (*stuvate*)), a house (वरूथं (*varūthaṃ*)), a warder-off of what is undesired, or else a house to be chosen.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वरुण (*varuṇa*)—O god Varuṇa
- यः (*yaḥ*)—the Vasiṣṭha who
- त्वां (*tvāṃ*)—in regard to you
- आगांसि (*āgāṃsi*)—offences
- कृणवत् (*kṛṇavat*)—though he has committed
- ते (*te*)—to you
- नित्यः (*nityaḥ*)—eternal
- आपिः सन् (*āpiḥ san*)—being a kinsman
- सखा (*sakhā*)—let him be your friend
- प्रियः (*priyaḥ*)—and an object of your love
- यक्षिन् (*yakṣin*)—O worshipful Varuṇa
- ते (*te*)—we who belong to you
- एनस्वंतः (*enasvaṃtaḥ*)—being sinful
- मा भुजेम (*mā bhujema*)—do not wish to enjoy happiness
- विप्रः (*vipraḥ*)—you, the wise
- स्तुवते (*stuvate*)—to the praiser Vasiṣṭha
- वरूधं (*varūdhaṃ*)—a house that destroys what is undesired
- यंधि (*yaṃdhi*)—give

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, let that Vasiṣṭha who has committed offences in regard to you, being your eternal kinsman, be your friend and an object of your love. O worshipful Varuṇa, we who belong to you do not wish to enjoy happiness while being sinful. You, the wise one, give to the praiser Vasiṣṭha a house that destroys what is undesired.

##### English translation
May he your unvarying kin, who was ever dear, though committing offences against you, still be your friend; adorable Varuna, offending you let us not enjoy (happiness); but do you, who are wise, bestow on your worshipper a secure abode.

#### Ṛk 7

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ध्रु॒वासु॑ त्वा॒सु क्षि॒तिषु॑ क्षि॒यंतो॒ व्य१॒ं॑स्मत्पाशं॒ वरु॑णो  
> मुमोचत् ।  
> अवो॑ वन्वा॒ना अदि॑तेरु॒पस्था॑द्य॒यं पा॑त स्व॒स्तिभिः॒ सदा॑ नः ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ध्रुवासु । त्वा । आसु । क्षितिषु । क्षियंतः । वि । अस्मत् । पाशं । वरुणः । मुमोचत् ।  
> अवः । वन्वानाः । आदितेः । उपस्थात् । ययं । पात । स्वस्तिभिः । सदा । नः ॥७॥

### Printed p. 83

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अत्र ऋग्विधानं । ध्रुवासु त्वासु क्षितिषु जपन् बंधात्पमुच्यते । तिष्ठनात्मा
> जपेदेतां विशाचस्तं न गच्छति । ऋग्वि. २-२८ । इति ॥
> ध्रुवासु नित्यास्वासु दृश्यमानासु क्षितिषु भूमिषु क्षियंतो निवसंतो
> वयं हे वरुण त्वा त्वां स्तुम इति शेषः । स च वरुणोऽस्मदस्मत्तः पाशं बंधकं
> पापु नि मुमोचत् । मोचयेत् तथादितेरखंडनीयायाः पृथिव्या उपस्थादुपस्थानादवो रक्षणं वरुणेन दत्तं वन्वानां संभजमाना वयं स्याम।
> अन्यद्गतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

Here is the Ṛgvidhāna rule: one who mutters the verse beginning with "ध्रुवासु (*dhruvāsu*)" is freed from bondage; one who mutters it standing, with his mind (collected), is not approached by a piśāca. (Ṛgvi. 2-28) **NOTE**
We who dwell in these constant earths that are seen, O Varuṇa, praise you (to be supplied). May that Varuṇa release from us the binding noose. Likewise may we, being sharers in the protection (अवः (*avaḥ*)) given by Varuṇa from the lap (उपस्थात् (*upasthāt*)) of Aditi, the undivided earth, be so. The rest has been explained.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- (O Varuṇa) ध्रुवासु (*dhruvāsu*)—in these eternally abiding
- आसु क्षितिसु (*āsu kṣitisu*)—earths
- क्षियंतः (*kṣiyaṃtaḥ*)—we who dwell
- त्वा (*tvā*)—praise you
- वरुणः (*varuṇaḥ*)—that same Varuṇa
- अस्मत् (*asmat*)—from us
- पाशं (*pāśaṃ*)—the bond
- वि मुमोचत् (*vi mumocat*)—let him release
- अदितेः (*aditeḥ*)—of the earth
- उपस्थात् (*upasthāt*)—from the neighbourhood
- अवः (*avaḥ*)—the protection given by Varuṇa
- वन्वानाः (*vanvānāḥ*)—may we enjoy (O Varuṇa and the other gods)
- यूयं (*yūyaṃ*)—you
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- सदा (*sadā*)—always
- स्वस्तिभिः (*svastibhiḥ*)—with blessings
- पात (*pāta*)—protect

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, we who dwell in these eternally abiding earths praise you. May that same Varuṇa release the bond from us. May we enjoy the protection given by Varuṇa from the neighbourhood of the earth. O Varuṇa and the other gods, protect us always with blessings.

##### English translation
Whilst dwelling in these durable worlds, may Varuna loose our bonds: may we be enjoyers of the protection (which has been given by him) from the lap of Aditi, and you (gods), ever cherish us with blessings.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the Ṛgvidhāna, a work attributed to Śaunaka, it is said as follows about this verse-part: ध्रुवासु त्वासु क्षितिषु जपन् बंधात्पमुच्यते । शिष्ठनात्रौ जपेदेतां पिशाचस्तं न गच्छति ॥ (*dhruvāsu tvāsu kṣitiṣu japan baṃdhātpamucyate | śiṣṭhanātrau japedetāṃ piśācastaṃ na gacchati ‖*) (Ṛgvidhāna 2-319). If one mutters this verse-part beginning प्रभाशु श्वासु (*prabhāśu śvāsu*) he becomes freed from bondage. **NOTE**  If this verse-part is muttered, piśācas and the like do not come near him; that is, he suffers no harm from piśācas and the like.

## Sūkta 89 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> मो षु वरुणेति पंचर्चमेकोनविंशं सूक्तं वारुणं । अंत्या जगती
> शिष्टाश्चतस्रो गायत्रः। तथा चानुक्रांतं । मो षु पंच गायत्रं जगत्त्यंतमिति ॥
> गतो विनियोगः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The hymn मो षु वरुणेति (*mo ṣu varuṇeti*) has five ṛks, is the nineteenth hymn (of the anuvāka), and is addressed to Varuṇa. The last verse is in Jagatī metre; the remaining four are in Gāyatrī. So the Anukramaṇī says: मो षु पंच गायत्रं जगत्त्यंतमिति (*mo ṣu paṃca gāyatraṃ jagattyaṃtamiti*). The use in ritual has been given.

##### Anuvāda

This hymn मोषु वरुण (*moṣu varuṇa*) is the nineteenth hymn in the fifth anuvāka. It has five ṛks. Vasiṣṭha is the seer of this hymn, Varuṇa the deity. The last ṛk of this hymn is in the Jagatī metre; the other four ṛks are in the Gāyatrī metre. In the Anukramaṇī it is said मोषु पंच गायत्रं जगत्त्यंतं (*moṣu paṃca gāyatraṃ jagattyaṃtaṃ*). The use in ritual has already been stated.

- Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 5; Sūkta 89
- Aṣṭaka 5; Adhyāya 6; Varga 11
- Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 5
- Seer: Vasiṣṭha
- Deity: Varuṇa
- Metre: 1–4 Gāyatrī; 5 Jagatī

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> मो षु व॑रुण मृ॒न्मयं॑ गृ॒हं रा॑जन्न॒हु ग॑मं ।  
> मृ॒ळा सु॑क्षत्र मृ॒ळय॑ ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> मो इति । सु । वरुण । मृत्मयं । गृहं । राजन् । अहं । गमं ।  
> मृळ । सुक्षत्र । मृळय ॥ १ ॥

### Printed p. 84
#### Ṛk 1 (continued)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे राजन्नीश्वर वरुण त्वदीयं मृन्मयं मृदादिभिर्निर्मितं गृहं मो मा
> उ मैवाहं गमं गतोऽस्मि । अपि तु सु शोभनं सुवर्णमयमेव त्वदीयं
> गृहं प्राप्नवानि । स त्वं मां मृळ । सुखय। हे सुक्षत्र शोभनधन वरुण मृळय।
> उपदयां च कुरु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O king, lord Varuṇa, may I never go to your house made of clay and similar materials. Let me instead reach your fine house, made wholly of gold. Make me happy, and O Sukṣatra, Varuṇa of fine wealth, be gracious: show compassion."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- राजन् (*rājan*)—O Varuṇa, the lord
- वरुण (*varuṇa*)—O god Varuṇa
- मृन्मयं (*mṛnmayaṃ*)—made of clay
- गृहं (*gṛhaṃ*)—to the house
- अहं (*ahaṃ*)—I
- मो गमं (*mo gamaṃ*)—let me not go
- सु (*su*)—may I go to the excellent house of gold
- सुक्षत्र (*sukṣatra*)—O god possessing excellent wealth
- मृळ (*mṛḷa*)—make us happy
- मृळय (*mṛḷaya*)—show compassion

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, lord, do not let me go to a house built of clay; let me go to the house made of gold. O god of excellent wealth, make us happy. Show compassion to us.

##### English translation
May I never go, royal Varuna, to a house made of clay: grant me happiness, possessor of wealth, grant me happiness.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यदेमि॑ प्रस्थु॒रन्नि॑व॒ दृति॒र्न ध्मा॒तो अ॑द्रिवः ।  
> मृ॒ळा सु॑कक्षत्र मृ॒ळय॑ ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । एमि । प्रस्सुरन् इव । दृतिः । न । ध्मातः । अद्र्विवः ।  
> मृळ । सुक्षेत्र । मृळय ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सायुजभाष्यसहिता [ मं. ७. आ. ५. सू. ८९.
> ॥ सायुजभास्कं ॥
> हे अद्रिव आयुधवन्वरुण यद्यदा प्रस्फरन्निव शैत्येन प्रविचलन्निव त्वद्भया.
> द्वेपमानो दृतिर्न दृतिरिव ध्मातो वायुना पूर्णः सन् त्वया बद्धोऽहमेमि
> गच्छामि तदानीं मृळ । सुखय । हे सुक्षेत्र सुधन मृळय । उपदयां कुरु ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Adrivat, weapon-bearing Varuṇa, when I go, trembling from cold as it were, quivering in fear of you, like a skin (dṛti) filled with wind, bound by you, then make me happy. O Sukṣetra, of good wealth, show compassion."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अद्रिवः (*adrivaḥ*)—O Varuṇa, bearer of weapons
- ध्मातः (*dhmātaḥ*)—filled with wind
- दृतिः न (*dṛtiḥ na*)—like a leather bag (bellows)
- प्रस्सुरन्निव (*prassuranniva*)—with the humility of "perhaps I am trembling (from fear and devotion on seeing you)"
- यत् (*yat*)—when
- एमि (*emi*)—I approach you (then)
- मृळ (*mṛḷa*)—make us happy
- सुक्षत्र (*sukṣatra*)—O Varuṇa of excellent wealth
- मृळय (*mṛḷaya*)—show compassion to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, bearer of weapons, when I approach you, trembling, with the humility of one who trembles from fear and devotion on seeing you, like a leather bag (bellows) filled with wind, then make us happy. O Varuṇa of excellent wealth, show compassion to us.

##### English translation
When, Varuna, I am throbbing as if (with awe) like an inflated skin, grant me happiness, possessor of wealth, grant me happiness.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> क्रत्वः॑ समह दी॒नता॑ प्रती॒षं ज॑गमा शुचे:  
> मृ॒ळा सु॑क्षत्र मृ॒ळय॑ ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> क्रत्त्वः । समह । दीनता । प्रतिईपं । जगम । शुचे ।  
> मृळ । सुक्षेत्र । मृळय ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अ. ५. अ. ६. व- ११.] ऋग्वेदसंहिता '87

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे समह सधन शुचे स्वभावतो निर्मल वरुण दीनता दीनतयाशक्ततया
> क्रत्वः कर्मणः कर्तव्यत्वेन विहितस्य श्रौतस्मार्तादिलक्षणस्य प्रतीपं प्रतिकूलमननुष्ठानं जगम । प्राप्तवानस्मि । आत एव त्वया बद्धः । तादृशं मां मृळ । सुखय ।
> अन्यद्गतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Samaha, possessor of wealth, O Śuci, naturally pure Varuṇa: through weakness (dīnatā) I have come to the contrary, to the non-performance, of the rite enjoined as a duty, of the śrauta, smārta and similar kinds. For that very reason I am bound by you. Make me, such as I am, happy." The rest is as before.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- समह (*samaha*)—O one endowed with wealth
- शुचे (*śuce*)—O self-pure Varuṇa
- दीनता (*dīnatā*)—whether through weakness or through ignorance
- क्रत्वः (*kratvaḥ*)—to the obligatory rites, śrauta, smārta and the rest
- प्रतीपं (*pratīpaṃ*)—contrary
- जगम (*jagama*)—even if I have acted
- मृळ (*mṛḷa*)—give me happiness
- सुकृत्र (*sukṛtra*)—O Varuṇa of excellent wealth
- मृळय (*mṛḷaya*)—show compassion to us

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, possessor of wealth and self-pure, even if, through weakness or through ignorance, I have acted contrary to the enjoined obligatory rites of the śrauta, smārta and other kinds, give me happiness. O Varuṇa of excellent wealth, show compassion to us.

##### English translation
Opulent and pure (Varuna), if through infirmity I have done what is contrary (to the law), yet grant me happiness, possessor of wealth, grant me happiness.

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> अ॒सां मध्ये॑ तस्थि॒वांसं॒ तृष्णा॑ विदज्जरि॒तारं॑ ।  
> मृ॒ळा सु॑क्षेत्र मृ॒ळय॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> अपां । मध्ये । तस्थि5वांसं । तृष्णा । अविदत् । जरितारं ।  
> मृळ । सुक्षेत्र । मृळय ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> ५८ सायणभाव्यसहिता [मं ७ अ. ५ सू. ८९

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> अपां समुद्राणामुदकानां मध्ये तस्थिवांसं स्थितवंतमपि जरितारं तव
> स्तोतारं मा तृष्णा पिपासाविदत् । आप्तवती । लवणोत्कटस्य सामुद्रजलस्य पानानर्हत्वात् । अतस्तादृशं मां मृळ । सुखय । अन्यद्धतं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"Though I, your worshipper, stand in the midst of the waters, the seas, thirst has found (reached) me, since the water of the sea, being full of salt, is unfit for drinking. Therefore make me, such as I am, happy." The rest is as before.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अपां (*apāṃ*)—of the waters of the sea
- मध्यॆ (*madhyè*)—in the middle
- तस्थिवांसं (*tasthivāṃsaṃ*)—even though standing
- जरितारं (*jaritāraṃ*)—me, your worshipper
- तृष्णा (*tṛṣṇā*)—thirst
- अविदत् (*avidat*)—has pervaded
- सुक्षत्र (*sukṣatra*)—O Varuṇa of excellent wealth
- मृळ (*mṛḷa*)—make me happy
- मृळय (*mṛḷaya*)—show compassion to me

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, though I stand in the midst of the waters of the sea, thirst has pervaded me, your worshipper, because they are unfit for drinking. O Varuṇa of excellent wealth, make me happy. Show compassion to me.

##### English translation
Thirst distresses (me) your worshipper in the midst of the waters: grant me happiness, possessor of wealth, grant me happiness.

#### Ṛk 5

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> यत्किं चे॒दं व॑रुण॒ दैव्ये॒ जने॑ऽभिद्रो॒हं म॑नु॒ष्या॒३॒॑श्चरा॑मसि ।  
> अचि॑त्ती॒ यत्तन॒ धर्मा॑ युयोपि॒म मा न॒स्तस्मा॒देन॑सो  
> देव रीरिषः ॥ ५ ।

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> यत् । किं । च । इदं । वरुण । दैव्ये । जने । अभिद्रोहं । मनुष्याः । चरामसि ।  
> अचित्ती । यत् । तव । धर्म । युयोपिम । मा । नः । तस्मात् । एनसः । देव ।  
> रिरिषः ॥ ५ ॥

### Printed p. 86

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> देनसुवां हविःषु वारुणस्य हविषो यत्किं चेदमिति याज्या । सूत्रितं च ।
> यत्किंचेदं वरुण दैव्ये जन उप ते स्तोमान्सशुपा इवाकरमिति द्वे ` । आ. ४-११ ।
> इति ॥
> हे वरुण वैवै देवसमूहरूपे जने यदिदं किंचनाभिद्रोहमपकारजातं मनुष्या वयु चरामसि चरामः निर्वर्तयामः । तथाचित्त्यचित्त्याज्ञानेन
> तव त्वदीयं यद्धर्म धारकं कर्म युयोपिम वयं विमोहितवंतः । हे देव
> तस्मादेनसः पापानोऽस्मान्मा रीरिषः । मा हिंसीः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the Devasu offerings (देनसुवां हविःषु (*denasuvāṃ haviḥṣu*)), the Varuṇa oblation has यत्किं चेदं (*yatkiṃ cedaṃ*) as its yājyā, and the sūtra (Ā. 4-11) prescribes it, "the two (ṛks) beginning यत्किंचेदं (*yatkiṃcedaṃ*)". "O Varuṇa, whatever offence, whatever injury, we men commit against the people who are the host of the gods; and whatever supporting rite (dharma) of yours we have bewildered (confused) through ignorance (acitti): O god, from that sin do not harm us."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वरुण (*varuṇa*)—O Varuṇa
- दैव्ये (*daivye*)—belonging to the host of the gods
- जने (*jane*)—in the matter of the people
- इदं यत्किंचन (*idaṃ yatkiṃcana*)—whatever that can be specifically pointed out
- अभिद्रोहं (*abhidrohaṃ*)—an injurious act
- मनुष्याः (*manuṣyāḥ*)—we men
- चरामसि (*carāmasi*)—even if we have committed
- अचित्ती (*acittī*)—through ignorance
- तव (*tava*)—belonging to your ordinance
- यत् (*yat*)—whatever
- धर्म (*dharma*)—the law that upholds the world
- युयोपिम (*yuyopima*)—even if we have transgressed
- देव (*deva*)—O Varuṇa, divine in nature
- तस्मादेनसः (*tasmādenasaḥ*)—from such sins (on account of them)
- नः (*naḥ*)—us
- मा रीरिषः (*mā rīriṣaḥ*)—do not harm

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O Varuṇa, even if we men have committed any injurious act whatever, that can be specifically pointed out, against the people belonging to the host of the gods, or have through ignorance transgressed any world-upholding law belonging to your ordinance, O Varuṇa, divine in nature, do not harm us on account of such sins.

##### English translation
Whatever the offence which we men commit, Varuna, against divine beings, whatever law of you we may through ignorance violate, do not you, divine Varuna, punish us on account of that iniquity.

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

Among the eight homas offered to various deities, called Devasuvas, this ṛk beginning यत्किंचेदं (*yatkiṃcedaṃ*) is to be recited as the yājyā mantra in the havirhoma offered to the deity Varuṇa; this is set out in the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra

### Printed p. 90
by the sūtra यत्किंचेदं वरुण दैव्ये जन उप ते स्तोमान्बशुषा इवा करमिति द्वे (*yatkiṃcedaṃ varuṇa daivye jana upa te stomānbaśuṣā ivā karamiti dve*) (Ā. 4-11).

## Sūkta 90 (Maṇḍala 7)

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> षष्ठेऽनुवाके पंचदश सूक्तानि । तत्र प्र वीरयेति सप्तर्चं प्रथमं सूक्तं
> वसिष्ठस्यार्षं त्रैष्टुभं वायव्यं । यासु पंचम्याद्यासु द्विवचनमस्ति ता ऐंद्रवायव्यः । तथा चानुक्रांतं। प्र वीरया सप्त वायव्यं हैंद्रश्च या द्विवदुक्ता
> इति ॥ तृतीये छंदोमे प्रउगशस्त्रे प्र वीरयेति वायव्यस्तृचः । सूत्रितं च ।
> प्र वीरया शुचयो दद्रिरे नां ते सत्येन मनसा दीध्यानाः । आ. ८.११ । इति ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

The sixth anuvāka has fifteen sūktas. The first of them, the sūkta of seven ṛks beginning प्र वीरया (*pra vīrayā*), has Vasiṣṭha as its seer, Triṣṭubh as its metre, and Vāyu as its deity. The ṛks from the fifth on, in which a dual occurs, are addressed to Indra and Vāyu. So the Anukramaṇī says: प्र वीरया सप्त वायव्यं हैंद्रश्च या द्विवदुक्ता इति (*pra vīrayā sapta vāyavyaṃ haiṃdraśca yā dvivaduktā iti*). In the third Chandoma day, in the Prauga-śastra, the three ṛks beginning प्र वीरया (*pra vīrayā*) form the Vāyu tṛca. The sūtra says: प्र वीरया शुचयो दद्रिरे नां ते सत्येन मनसा दीध्यानाः (*pra vīrayā śucayo dadrire nāṃ te satyena manasā dīdhyānāḥ*) (Ā. 8.11).

##### Anuvāda

In the sixth anuvāka of the seventh maṇḍala there are fifteen sūktas. Of these the sūkta प्र वीरया (*pra vīrayā*) is the first; it has seven ṛks. The seer of this sūkta is Vasiṣṭha, the deity Vāyu, the metre Triṣṭubh. The dual words in the fifth ṛk and in the ṛks that follow it indicate Indra and Vāyu. The Anukramaṇī says प्र वीरया सप्त वायव्यं हैंद्रश्च या द्विवदुक्ता (*pra vīrayā sapta vāyavyaṃ haiṃdraśca yā dvivaduktā*). At the time of reciting the third Chandoma mantras, when the Prauga-śastra mantras are recited, the three ṛks beginning प्र वीरया (*pra vīrayā*) are to be recited as the Vāyu tṛca; this is set out by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra प्र वीरया शुचयो दद्रिरे नां ते सत्येन मनसा दीध्यानाः (*pra vīrayā śucayo dadrire nāṃ te satyena manasā dīdhyānāḥ*) (Ā. 8.11).

Sūkta 90.
Maṇḍala 7; Anuvāka 5; Sūkta 90.
Aṣṭaka 5; Adhyāya 6; Varga 12.
Number of ṛks in the sūkta: 7.
Seer: Vasiṣṭha.
Deity: 1-4, Vāyu; 5-7, Indra and Vāyu.
Metre: Triṣṭubh.

#### Ṛk 1

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> प्र वी॑र॒या शुच॑यो दद्रिरे वामध्व॒र्युभि॒र्मधु॑मंतः  ॒॑
> सुतासः ।

### Printed p. 91

> वह वायो नियुतो याह्यच्छा पिबा सुतस्यांधसो  
> मदाय ॥ १ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> प्र । वीरया । शुचयः । दद्रिरे । वां । अध्वर्युभिः । मधुमंतः । सुतासः ।  
> वह । वायो इति । नियुतः । याहि । अच्छ । पिब । सुतस्य । अंधसः । मदाय ॥१॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> हे वायो वीरया वीराय विविधमीरयित्रे ! सुपां सुलुगिति चतुर्थ्या
> याजादेशः ॥ वां ते । व्यत्ययेन द्विवचनं ॥ तुभ्यं शुचयः शुद्धा मधुमंतो
> माधुर्योपेताः सुतासोऽभिषुताः सोमा ऐतरेयिभिर्नवमेऽहनि प्रउगेऽध्वर्युभिरध्वरस्य नेतृभिऋत्विग्भिः प्रदद्रिरे । प्रदीयंते ॥ दद दान इत्यस्यैतद्रूपं ॥ यत एवमतः कारणात् हे वायो नियुतो वडवा वह । रथं प्रापय ।
> तेन च रथेनाच्छ याहि । अस्मद्यज्ञमभिगच्छ । अभिगत्य च सुतस्याभिसुतस्यांधसोऽन्नस्य सोमलक्षणस्य स्वकीयं भागं पिब । किमर्थं । मदाय मदोत्पत्त्यर्थं ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

"O Vāyu, to you, vīrayā, who stir (īray) in manifold ways—the dative is replaced by the ending yāc, and vāṃ stands for te, a dual for a singular by exchange of number—the pure, sweet (honeyed) Soma juices, pressed, are offered by the Aitareyins, by the adhvaryus, the priests who lead the sacrifice, on the ninth day, in the Prauga. Since this is so, O Vāyu, harness your niyut mares, bring the chariot, and come with it towards us, to our sacrifice. Having come, drink your own share of the pressed Soma, the food, for exhilaration."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- वायो (*vāyo*)—O god Vāyu
- वीरया (*vīrayā*)—endowed with vigour
- नां (*nāṃ*)—towards you
- शुचयः (*śucayaḥ*)—pure
- मधुमंतः (*madhumaṃtaḥ*)—endowed with sweetness
- सुतासः (*sutāsaḥ*)—pressed Soma juices
- अध्वर्युभिः (*adhvaryubhiḥ*)—by the adhvaryus
- प्र दद्रिरे (*pra dadrire*)—are offered
- नियुतः (*niyutaḥ*)—your horses
- वह (*vaha*)—yoke to the chariot
- अच्छ (*accha*)—facing us
- याहि (*yāhi*)—be pleased to come
- सुतस्य (*sutasya*)—of what is pressed and ready
- अंधसः (*aṃdhasaḥ*)—of the portion of the food in the form of Soma
- मदाय (*madāya*)—for satisfaction
- पिब (*piba*)—drink

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O god Vāyu, towards you, who are endowed with vigour, pure, sweet, pressed Soma juices are offered by the adhvaryus. Yoke your horses to the chariot. Be pleased to come towards us. For your satisfaction, drink the portion of the food in the form of Soma that has been pressed and made ready.

### Printed p. 94

##### English translation
The sweet and pure Soma juices are offered to you, the hero Vayu, by the priests : therefore harness your Niyut steeds, come hither and drink of the effused Soma for your exhilaration.

#### Ṛk 2

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> ई॒शा॒नाय॒ प्रहु॑तिं॒ यस्त॒ आन॒ट्टुचिं॒ सोमं॑ शुचिपा॒स्तुभ्यं॑ वायो ।  
> कृ॒णोषि॒ तं मर्त्ये॑षु प्रश॒स्तं जा॒तोजा॑तो जायते  ॒॑
> वाज्यस्य ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> ईशानाय । प्रहुतिं । यः । ते । आनट् शुचिं । सोमं । शुचिपाः । तुभ्यं ।  
> वायो इति ।  
> कृणोषि । तं । मर्त्येषु प्रशस्तं । जातःजातः । जायते । वाजी । अस्य ॥ २ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> शुनासीरीये पर्वणि वायव्यस्य हविष ईशानायेति याज्या । सूत्रितं च ।
> स त्वं नो देव मनसेशानाय प्रहुतिं यस्त आनट् । आ २-२८ । इति ॥ वायव्ये
> पशावेषैव पशुपुरोडाशस्य याज्या । सूत्रितं च । ईशानाय प्रहुतिं यस्त आनट्
> प्र वो वायुं रथयुजं कृणुध्वं । आ ३-८ । इति ॥
> हे वायो ईशानायेश्वराय ते तुभ्यं त्वदर्थं प्रहुतिं प्रकृष्टामाहुतिं
> चरुपुरोडाशादिसाध्यां यो यजमान आनट् प्राप्नोत् । दद्यादित्यर्थः । तथा हे
> शुचिपाः शुद्धस्य सोमस्य पातर्वायो तुभ्यं शुचिं शुद्धं सोमं च यः प्रयच्छति मर्त्येषु मनुष्येषु मध्यॆ तं यजमानं प्रशस्तं मुख्यं कृणोषि ।
> करोषि । स च जातो जातः सर्वत्र प्रादुर्भूतः प्रख्यातः सन् वाज्यस्य प्राप्तव्यस्य
> धनस्य प्राप्तये जायते । अवकल्पते । सर्वं धनं लभत इत्यर्थः ॥
> अ. ५.- अ. ६. प. १२.] कविगैदसंहिता ९३

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the Śunāsīrīya parvan, ईशानाय (*īśānāya*) is the yājyā for the Vāyavya oblation; the sūtra says स त्वं नो देव मनसेशानाय प्रहुतिं यस्त आनट् (*sa tvaṃ no deva manaseśānāya prahutiṃ yasta ānaṭ*) (Ā. 2-28). In the Vāyavya animal sacrifice this same ṛk is the yājyā of the animal's purodāśa; the sūtra says ईशानाय प्रहुतिं यस्त आनट् प्र वो वायुं रथयुजं कृणुध्वं (*īśānāya prahutiṃ yasta ānaṭ pra vo vāyuṃ rathayujaṃ kṛṇudhvaṃ*) (Ā. 3-8). "O Vāyu, to you, the lord (īśāna), the sacrificer who offers (āna, reaches) an excellent oblation (prahuti) prepared with caru, purodāśa and the like, i.e. gives it; and, O Śucipā, drinker of pure Soma, who gives you pure Soma: that sacrificer among mortals you make eminent, foremost. Being born and born, i.e. appearing and becoming renowned everywhere, he becomes fit for the attainment of the wealth that is to be attained (vājya); i.e. he obtains all wealth."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- पायो (*pāyo*)—O god Vāyu
- ईशानाय (*īśānāya*)—to you, the lord of all
- ते (*te*)—towards you
- प्रहुतिं (*prahutiṃ*)—the oblation accompanied by caru and purodāśa
- यः (*yaḥ*)—which sacrificer
- आनट् (*ānaṭ*)—offers (and moreover)
- शुचिपाः (*śucipāḥ*)—O Vāyu, drinker of pure Soma
- तुभ्यं (*tubhyaṃ*)—to you
- शुचि: (*śuci:*)—pure
- सोमं (*somaṃ*)—the Soma juice (whoever offers)
- तं (*taṃ*)—that sacrificer
- मर्त्येषु (*martyeṣu*)—among men
- प्रशस्तं (*praśastaṃ*)—as most eminent
- कृणोषि (*kṛṇoṣi*)—you make
- जातोजातः (*jātojātaḥ*)—that sacrificer, born everywhere, i.e. renowned
- वाज्यस्य (*vājyasya*)—for the attainment of wealth
- जायते (*jāyate*)—obtains fitness

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

O god Vāyu, whichever sacrificer offers to you, the lord of all, an oblation accompanied by caru and purodāśa; and O Vāyu, drinker of pure Soma, whoever offers you pure Soma juice, that sacrificer you make most eminent among men. That sacrificer, born everywhere, renowned in every place, obtains fitness for the attainment of full wealth.

##### English translation
Drinker of Soma juice, Vayu, you elevetes him who among mortals offers to you who are the lord, an excellent oblation, the pure Soma: repeatedly born, he is born for the acquirement of wealth.

#### Ṛk 3

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> रा॒ये नु यं ज॒ज्ञतू॒ रोद॑सी॒मे रा॒ये दे॒वी धि॒षणा॑  ॒
> धाति देवं ।  
> अध॑ वा॒युं नि॒युतः॑ सश्चत॒ स्वा उ॒त श्वे॒तं वसु॑धितिं निरे॒के॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> राये । नु । यं । जज्ञतुः । रोदसी इति । इमे इति । राये । देवी । धिषणा ।  
> धाति । देवं ।  
> अध । वायुं । नियुतः । सश्चत । स्वाः । उत । श्वेतं । वसुधितिं । निरेके ॥ ३ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> सारांशभाष्यसंहिता [ मं. ५-अ. ५. सं. ६०.

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> नियुत्वद्गुणविशिष्टवायुदेवताके पशौ राये नु यमिति पुरोडाशस्य
> सूत्रितं च । राये नु यं जज्जतू रोदसीमे प्र वायुमच्छा बृहती
> मनीषा । आ. ३-८ । इति ॥
> इमे रोदसी द्यावापृथिव्यौ यं वायुं राये धनार्थं नु क्षिप्रं जज्ञतुः
> जनयामासतुः तु देवं दानादिगुणयुक्तं वायुं देवी द्योतमाना धिषणा
> स्तुती राये धनार्थं धाति । धारयति । धनं यथा लभ्यते तथा प्रेरयतीत्यर्थः ।
> अधाधुनैवं स्तुता प्रवृत्तायां स्वाः स्वकीया नियुतो वडना रथवाहा वायुं
> सश्चत । सचंते । सेवंते । उतापि च श्वेतं शुभ्रवर्णं निरेके । नितरां रेको
> रिक्तता निरेकः । दारिद्रमित्यर्थः । तस्मिन्सति वसुधितिं वसूनां धातारं प्रदातारं
> तं वायुं नियुतोऽस्मद्यज्ञं प्रापयंतीति शेषः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the animal sacrifice whose deity is Vāyu endowed with niyuts, राये नु यं (*rāye nu yaṃ*) is the yājyā of the purodāśa; the sūtra says राये नु यं जज्जतू रोदसीमे प्र वायुमच्छा बृहती मनीषा (*rāye nu yaṃ jajjatū rodasīme pra vāyumacchā bṛhatī manīṣā*) (Ā. 3-8). "These two worlds, heaven and earth, quickly begot Vāyu for the sake of wealth. The shining praise (dhiṣaṇā) holds (dhāti) the divine Vāyu, who is endowed with the quality of giving, for wealth; i.e. it impels him so that wealth is obtained. Now, when the praise has thus begun, his own niyuts, the chariot-drawing mares, attend (saścata) Vāyu. And in the nireka, i.e. emptiness, poverty, the niyuts bring to our sacrifice that Vāyu, the white, the vasudhiti, the bestower of wealth."

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- यं (*yaṃ*)—which Vāyu
- इमे रोदसी (*ime rodasī*)—these two, heaven and earth
- नु (*nu*)—quickly
- राये (*rāye*)—for the giving of wealth
- जज्ञतुः (*jajñatuḥ*)—brought into being
- देवं (*devaṃ*)—that same Vāyu, endowed with giving and other qualities
- देवी (*devī*)—shining
- धिषणा (*dhiṣaṇā*)—praise
- राये (*rāye*)—for the sake of wealth
- धाति (*dhāti*)—holds
- अध (*adha*)—at the very time of reciting the praise
- स्वाः (*svāḥ*)—of that same Vāyu
- नियुतः (*niyutaḥ*)—the horses
- वायुं (*vāyuṃ*)—Vāyu
- सश्चत (*saścata*)—attend
- उत (*uta*)—and
- श्वेतं (*śvetaṃ*)—the white-coloured
- वसुधितिं (*vasudhitiṃ*)—Vāyu, the giver of wealth
- निरेके (*nireke*)—they bring to the region stricken by poverty

##### भावार्थ (Bhāvārtha)

Vāyu, whom these two, heaven and earth, quickly brought into being for the giving of wealth, that same Vāyu, endowed with giving and other qualities, whom the shining praise holds for wealth: the horses of that same Vāyu, at the very time of this recitation of praise, attending him, bring the god Vāyu, white-coloured and the giver of wealth, to the region stricken by poverty.

##### English translation
His own niyut steeds bear to the place of poverty the white-
complexioned dispenser of wealth, Vayu, whom heaven and earth bore for
the sake of riches, whom the divine language of praise sustains as a deity
for the sake of riches.

### Printed p. 95

##### विशेषविषयगळु (Special Topics)

In the animal sacrifice offered to the deity called Niyutvān Vāyu, this ṛk beginning राये नु यं (*rāye nu yaṃ*) is to be recited as the yājyā mantra of the purodāśa homa; this is set out by the Āśvalāyana Śrauta Sūtra राये नु यं जज्ञतू रोदसीमे प्र वायुमच्छा बृहती मनीषा (*rāye nu yaṃ jajñatū rodasīme pra vāyumacchā bṛhatī manīṣā*) (Ā. 3-8).

#### Ṛk 4

##### ॥ संहितापाठः ॥ (Saṃhitā-pāṭha)

> उ॒च्छन्नु॒षसः॑ सु॒दिना॑ अरि॒प्रा उ॒रु ज्योति॑र्वि विदु॒र्दिध्या॑नाः ।  
> गव्यं॑ चिदू॒र्वमु॒तिजो॒ वि व॑व्र॒स्तेषा॒वनु॑ प्र॒दिवः॑ सस्रु॒रापः॑ ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ पदपाठः ॥ (Pada-pāṭha)

> उच्छन् । उषसः । सुदिनाः । अरिप्राः । उरु । ज्योतिः । विविदुः । दीध्यानाः ।  
> गव्यं । चित् । ऊर्वं । उशिजः । वि । वव्रुः । तेषां । अनु । प्रदिवः । सस्रुः ।  
> आपः ॥ ४ ॥

##### ॥ सायणभाष्यम् ॥ (Sāyaṇa-bhāṣya)

> द्वितीये छंदोमे प्रउगशस्त्रे वायव्यतृचस्योचच्छन्नुषस इति तृतीया ।
> सूत्रितं च । उच्छन्नुषसः सुदिना अरिप्रा इत्येकपातिन्यः । आ. ८-१० । इति ॥
> येऽंगिरसो वायुमस्तोषत तेषामरिप्राः पापरहिता उषसः सुदिनाः
> शोभनदिनस्य हेतुभूताः सत्य उच्छन् । औच्छन् । व्यवासयन् । ते च दीध्याना
> दीप्यमानाः संत उरु विस्तीर्णं ज्योतिः सूर्याख्यं विविदुः। वायोः प्रसादादलभंत । अपि चोशिजः कामयमानास्तेऽंगिरसो गव्यं चिद्गॊसंघरूपमप्यूर्वं
> धनं पणिभिरपहृतं वि वव्रुः । व्यवृण्वन् । अलभंत । तथा तेषामंगिरसामर्थाय
> प्रदिवः पुराण्य आपोऽनु ससुः । अन्वसरन् । अन्वगच्छन् । हिताहिताचरणपरा आसन्नित्यर्थः । अवरकस्यासुरस्य वायुना हननादसुरेणाहृतानुषःप्रभृतीन् पुनर्लब्धवंत इत्यर्थः ॥

##### Gist of the bhāṣya

In the second Chandoma day, in the Prauga-śastra, of the Vāyavya tṛca, this is the third ṛk; the sūtra says उच्छन्नुषसः सुदिना अरिप्रा इत्येकपातिन्यः (*ucchannuṣasaḥ sudinā ariprā ityekapātinyaḥ*) (Ā. 8-10). The Aṅgirases who praised Vāyu: for them the sinless dawns, causes of a good day, dawned. Shining, they found the wide light called the Sun, through Vāyu's favour. And desiring, those Aṅgirases won the abundance of wealth, in the form of herds of cows, that the Paṇis had carried off. For their sake the ancient waters flowed along (followed); they were intent on acting for good and ill. Since the lowly asura was slain by Vāyu, they regained the dawns and other things carried off by the asura.

##### प्रतिपदार्थ (Pratipadārtha)

- अरिप्राः (*ariprāḥ*)—sinless
- उषसः (*uṣasaḥ*)—the dawns (for the Aṅgirases, the worshippers of Vāyu)
- सुदिनाः (*sudināḥ*)—as the cause of excellent days
- उच्छन् (*ucchan*)—appeared
